# ExamForge — Member 1 REST API Specification & Endpoint Catalog
## Comprehensive Protocol and Payload Reference Guide

---

### ENDPOINT SPECIFICATION 1: API CONTRACT 1

**Endpoint URL:** `/api/auth/v1/contract-spec-1/`
**HTTP Method:** `POST / GET / PATCH`
**Authorized Roles:** `ADMIN`, `FACULTY`, `EXAM_STAFF`

```json
{
  "endpoint_id": "EP-M1-0001",
  "protocol_version": "v1.2",
  "description": "API contract specification for Member 1 authentication and faculty operations.",
  "rate_limit": "60 requests per minute"
}
```
